We present ChannelGuard, a training-free defense-in-depth framework placing information-bottleneck gates on every inter-agent channel; each scores channel text against an adversarial phrase bank by embedding similarity and deterministically passes, compresses, or blocks it, adding no LLM call, while an attribution method records which layer stopped each attack. ChannelGuard's tool-output gate blocks Tool Poisoning 30 of 30 at the application layer, identically across Azure GPT-5, Anthropic Sonnet 4.5, and Anthropic Haiku 4.5, whereas the undefended pipeline shifts entirely across backends; it also lowers Prompt Injection attack success by half (0.333 to 0.167) and preserves GSM8K accuracy exactly (0.867). White-box adaptive paraphrase evades every embedding gate, where a perturb-and-vote baseline does better. An extended appendix adds baselines, ablations, sweeps, a benign-preservation analysis, and a judge audit (kappa = 0.900), at a total cost of 47.36 USD.

</details>


### [68] [ChainWatch: A Kill Chain-Aligned Sequential Detection Framework for Multi-Step Attacks in MCP-Based AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19432)
*Om Narayan,Rashmi Jyoti,Ramkinker Singh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出ChainWatch，一种面向模型上下文协议（MCP）智能体的序列化安全检测框架，用于识别由多个表面正常的工具调用组合而成的多步骤攻击链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP使智能体能够访问外部工具、数据库和服务，但现有逐调用检测机制通常孤立评估单次操作，难以发现跨步骤、渐进式且具有上下文关联的攻击行为。

Method: ChainWatch以六阶段攻击链刻画会话中的攻击演进过程，使用隐马尔可夫模型对工具调用序列进行分类，并在会话呈现跨阶段可疑推进时触发检测规则。框架还定义了直接序列、间接提示注入链和混合多阶段场景的威胁模型，并从工具交互中提取20维行为特征。

Result: 作者通过安全研究文献中的五个代表性攻击场景验证框架，结果表明ChainWatch能够识别传统逐调用安全机制难以捕获的攻击链。摘要未提供具体的检测准确率、误报率、对比基线或运行开销数据。

Conclusion: 该工作将MCP智能体安全检测从单次调用审查扩展到会话级序列建模，为发现跨工具、跨步骤的复合威胁提供了结构化方法。其实际有效性仍需通过大规模真实数据、与现有检测器的定量比较及对规避策略的鲁棒性评估进一步验证。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is an open-source standard that allows AI agents to connect to external tools, databases, and services. While this connectivity enables powerful agent capabilities, it also introduces multi-step attacks that existing per-call defenses cannot reliably detect. Attackers can compose individually benign tool invocations into malicious sequences that evade isolated inspection. This paper presents ChainWatch, a sequential detection framework for identifying multi-step attacks in MCP-based AI agent systems. ChainWatch models attack progression using a six-stage kill chain and applies a Hidden Markov Model (HMM) to classify tool-call sequences. Detection rules are triggered when a session exhibits suspicious progression across multiple stages. The framework is supported by a structured threat model covering direct sequential attacks, indirect prompt injection chains, and hybrid multi-stage attacks. A 20-dimensional feature extraction schema captures behavioral signals from tool interactions. We demonstrate the approach using five representative attack scenarios from the security literature, showing how ChainWatch detects attack chains that evade traditional per-call security mechanisms.

</details>


### [69] [Building Trust in Autonomous Commerce: A Verifiable Global Event Timeline and AI-Ready Fraud Intelligence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19436)
*Rajat Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向智能体商业协议的可验证全局事件时间线，通过规范化事件、确定性批处理、Merkle追加承诺和区块链锚定，实现跨域事件的可互操作、可审计、防篡改与可验证时序记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AP2、ACP等智能体商业协议虽支持安全的智能体发起交易，但缺乏跨异构域的统一审计机制、篡改可检测的事件证据，以及无需依赖同步时钟的可信事件先后顺序。该缺口也限制了风险标记溯源和AI训练数据谱系的可信复现。

Method: 方法由四部分构成：1）采用规范事件模式与确定性序列化，保证相同事件具有一致表示；2）使用确定性批处理规则形成可复现事件顺序，避免依赖全局时钟同步；3）以Merkle树对批次进行追加式承诺，支持对单个事件的对数复杂度包含证明；4）将批次根锚定至区块链，建立可验证的时间性防篡改骨干。在此基础上，论文设计了经密码学签名的风险标记，将风险标签与已锚定证据绑定；同时提出数据集谱系模型，用于构建可复现、可检测篡改的AI训练数据流水线。

Result: 原型实验表明：构建包含50,000个事件的Merkle树耗时47毫秒；端到端验证时间低于0.013毫秒，且基本不随批次规模增长；包含证明大小从1,000个事件时的320字节增长至50,000个事件时的512字节，符合对数增长特征；在50,000个事件规模下，Merkle验证相较线性扫描快14.4倍。

Conclusion: 该方案以确定性排序、Merkle证明和区块链锚定为核心，为智能体商业活动提供了可扩展的跨域审计与证据溯源基础设施。实验结果支持其在大规模事件验证中的效率优势，但摘要未说明锚定频率、链上成本、跨域身份信任、恶意事件源输入及实际部署环境下的性能，因此这些仍是评估系统工程可行性与安全边界的关键问题。

Abstract: Agentic commerce protocols such as AP2 and ACP define mechanisms for secure agent-initiated transactions but do not provide interoperable, tamper-evident auditability or verifiable temporal ordering of events across heterogeneous domains. This paper addresses these gaps by proposing a verifiable global event timeline for agentic commerce, constructed from four core components: canonical event schemas that enforce deterministic serialization, deterministic batch formation ensuring reproducible ordering without reliance on synchronized clocks, Merkle-based append-only commitments providing logarithmic-cost inclusion proofs, and blockchain anchoring establishing a tamper-evident temporal backbone. Building on this infrastructure, we introduce a cryptographically signed fraud marker that binds risk labels to anchored evidence through an unforgeable provenance chain, and a dataset lineage model enabling reproducible, tamper-evident AI training pipelines. Empirical results from a prototype implementation demonstrate: Merkle tree construction processes 50,000 events in 47 milliseconds; end-to-end verification completes in under 0.013 milliseconds regardless of batch size; inclusion proof sizes grow logarithmically from 320 bytes at 1,000 events to 512 bytes at 50,000 events; and Merkle-based verification outperforms linear scan by 14.4x at 50,000 events.

</details>


### [70] [Intelligent Disruption: Undetectable Attacks on Wireless Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19448)
*Han Jiang,Jifa Zhang,Hu Jin,Nan Zhao,Mingqian Liu,Yunfei Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向无线自编码通信系统的深度学习智能对抗攻击框架，通过多攻击者发射功率控制降低累积泄漏干扰，并利用条件生成对抗机制生成适应动态信道的扰动信号，以同时提升攻击隐蔽性、有效性和环境适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多攻击者并行实施对抗攻击时会产生累积泄漏干扰，增加攻击被检测的概率；同时，动态无线环境使固定攻击策略难以维持稳定效果。因此，需要一种能够协调多攻击者、降低可检测性并随信道条件自适应调整的攻击方法。

Method: 首先，针对多攻击者并行传输造成的累积泄漏干扰，构建基于深度神经网络的发射功率控制模型，通过调节各攻击者的发射功率减少干扰泄漏。其次，设计条件生成对抗攻击模型：生成器以攻击信道信息为条件输入，产生扰动信号；判别器在相同条件下区分受攻击接收信号与干净接收信号。通过对抗训练，生成器学习生成能够使受攻击信号更接近干净信号分布、且适应信道变化的扰动。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提框架相较于基准方法，在攻击隐蔽性、攻击强度以及对动态环境的适应性方面均具有更优表现。摘要未给出具体的性能增益、实验参数、基准模型名称或统计显著性结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将多攻击者功率协同控制与条件生成对抗学习结合，为动态无线通信场景中的自适应对抗攻击提供了统一方案。其主要贡献在于同时处理多攻击者泄漏干扰和信道动态变化问题；但仍需结合完整论文进一步验证模型的训练开销、信道信息获取假设、对不同通信体制的泛化能力及实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks can degrade the legitimate decision performance in wireless autoencoder communications. However, in complex scenarios with multiple adversaries, the cumulative leakage interference (CLI) caused by the multiple parallel attacks increases the chance of detecting the attacks, while dynamical environments also make the fixed attack strategies difficult to have stable effectiveness. To jointly enhance the undetectability, aggressivity and adaptability of adversarial attacks, we propose a deep learning based intelligent attack framework. Specifically, considering the CLI caused by the multiple parallel attacks, a deep neural network based transmit power control is established to reduce the interference leakage by regulating the transmit power of these adversaries, thereby improving the undetectability. Furthermore, to enhance the attack effectiveness and stability in the dynamic environment, the conditional generative adversarial attack is further developed. The generator takes the attack channel information as the conditional input to produce the perturbating signals to mislead the discriminator by making the attacked received signals resemble the clean received signals, while the discriminator distinguishes between the two under the same condition. Through the adversarial training, the generator can learn to create adaptive perturbating signals with enhanced attack performance. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework outperforms benchmarks in terms of attack undetectability, aggressivity and adaptability.

</details>


### [71] [Integrity of peer-to-peer distributed LLM inference under malicious nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19490)
*Mert Cihangiroglu,Antonino Nocera*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于点对点分布式大语言模型推理的输出完整性检测方法：将秘密金丝雀输入混入正常请求，通过比较节点输出激活与预先已知参考激活之间的偏差，识别篡改模型中间结果的节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在由多个独立参与方共同提供硬件的分布式推理场景中，任一节点都可能修改其负责层的输出，进而影响最终结果。现有基于可信硬件重算、已知答案陷阱或密码学承诺的方法，要么计算开销较高，要么仅检测严格一致性，难以适应正常硬件差异导致的良性输出波动。

Method: 用户预先选择少量正确激活已知的秘密金丝雀输入，并将其随机混入常规推理流量。由于节点无法区分金丝雀和真实请求，若其篡改输出，同样会影响金丝雀激活。方法以激活相对参考值的漂移为证据，将节点识别建模为区分良性硬件噪声分布与篡改所致漂移分布的概率检验，而非依赖单一固定阈值。

Result: 作者在408种预先确定指标与成功标准的实验配置中评估该检测器。结果显示，检测器在全部配置下均达到AUROC 1.0；对于每个金丝雀输入，均能将发生篡改的分片排名在所有良性分片之前。

Conclusion: 基于秘密金丝雀激活漂移的概率检测能够在无需对全部前向传播进行可信重算的条件下，有效验证分布式推理节点的输出完整性。该方法尤其适合存在自然硬件误差的异构参与者环境，并显示出极强的实验区分能力。

Abstract: Peer-to-peer distributed inference executes a Large Language Model (LLM) on pooled consumer hardware by spreading its layers across many nodes. Every request passes through nodes that are owned and controlled by multiple independent parties. However, in this setting, any party can tamper with the output of its layers to corrupt the end result. Recomputing the forward pass on trusted hardware can catch this, but it introduces additional computational cost. The scientific literature includes several prior integrity-checking approaches, such as known-answer traps for image classifiers and cryptographic commitments. However, these solutions test only the exact correctness and do not account for the ordinary variation that may arise between benign nodes. In this paper, we propose a method that checks the output integrity by measuring the variation in the activations that each node passes to the next. A peer who wants to use the network selects a small set of secret canary inputs whose correct activations are known in advance and mixes them into regular traffic. Because the peers cannot tell a canary from a real query, any tampering node corrupts them as well. The deviation from the known reference then reveals malicious activity: benign nodes exhibit only minor variation from hardware-induced noise, whereas tampered nodes deviate far more. We treat the identification of malicious nodes as a probabilistic test that separates two drift distributions, without relying on a fixed threshold. We study 408 configurations with metrics and success criteria fixed before any experiment ran; the detector reaches AUROC 1.0, correctly ranking the malicious shard above every benign shard on every canary in every configuration.

</details>


### [72] [When HTTP 402 Meets the Blockchain: Risks on Emerging x402 Payments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19545)
*Qinying Wang,Yong Yang,Yuan Chen,Shouling Ji,Mathias Payer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了现实世界中由第三方促进者（facilitator）支撑的 x402 支付协议部署安全性，发现所有受测促进者均存在关键安全规则违反，可能导致免费获取服务、商户资产损失、促进者资产被盗、服务中断及无限制手续费消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: x402 将支付证明验证与链上结算委托给促进者，使其成为众多商户共享的关键支付基础设施。该架构带来信任与验证集中化风险：单个促进者的实现缺陷可能同时影响大量独立商户和用户，但此前缺少针对真实部署的系统性安全评估。

Method: 作者提出促进者应满足的八项授权正确性与执行安全规则，并据此分析违规后果，归纳出四类攻击路径。随后构建半自动化黑盒测试工具，对15个主要 x402 促进者进行规模化检测；同时分析超过1.19亿笔 Base 与 Solana 链上交易，以测量协议采用情况、促进者集中度和生态风险指标。

Result: 测试显示，15个受测促进者全部违反至少一项安全规则。这些缺陷可被用于免费获得商品或服务、造成商户直接经济损失、转移促进者托管资产、消耗由赞助方支付的无上限链上费用，以及干扰支付服务。研究覆盖的促进者服务超过6万名卖家和36万名买家，表明问题具有显著的生态影响范围。作者已负责任地披露问题，相关方确认并部署了缓解措施。

Conclusion: x402 的促进者机制虽然简化了支付集成，却形成了高价值且高度集中的安全边界。论文表明，现实部署中的授权验证、付款绑定、结算执行及费用控制普遍不足。促进者应将八项安全规则作为实现与审计基线，并结合黑盒持续测试、严格的交易上下文绑定、幂等性与重放防护、资产权限最小化及费用上限机制，以降低系统性风险。

Abstract: x402 is an emerging payment protocol for Web APIs and autonomous AI agents. x402 extends HTTP 402 with a payment negotiation flow and delegates payment proof verification and on-chain settlement to third-party facilitators. As a result, facilitators serve as a shared payment infrastructure for many independent merchants. This centralizes trust and validation in one component, so a single flaw can affect many services. Despite rapid adoption by major vendors and economically meaningful mainnet activity, the security posture of real-world x402 deployments remains poorly characterized.
  We present the first systematic study of authorization correctness and execution safety in current facilitator-mediated x402 deployments in the wild, identifying eight security rules for facilitators as critical payment infrastructure. Based on our analysis of rule violations, we derive four new attack vectors, including Free Shopping, Asset Theft, Service Denial, and Gas Abuse. These attacks exploit weaknesses in the real-world facilitator and server implementations and cause severe harm, including direct financial loss to merchants, theft of facilitator-held assets, unbounded sponsor-paid gas/fees, and disruption of payment services. To assess the security of x402 deployments at scale, we propose a semi-automated black-box tool and apply it to 15 major x402 facilitators collectively used by over 60K sellers and 360K buyers. Alarmingly, we find violations in all evaluated facilitators. We responsibly disclosed our findings to the affected parties, who acknowledged the issues and adopted mitigations, including changes by Coinbase. Finally, we complement our controlled testing with an empirical measurement of over 119 million recent Base and Solana transactions, quantifying x402 adoption, facilitator centralization, and ecosystem-level risk indicators.

</details>


### [73] [Examining User Behavior and Cognitive Biases in Personal Password Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19586)
*Evelyn Crowe,Patralika Ghosh,Shreyas Kumar,Rebecca Schlegel,Rebecca Ward,Guofei Gu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究以行为经济学解释用户为何持续采用不安全的密码习惯，指出即时便利性通常压过长期安全收益，并为以用户为中心的认证设计提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管用户对网络安全风险的认知不断提升，弱密码、密码复用及忽视安全建议等行为仍普遍存在。研究旨在解释安全意识与实际安全行为之间的落差。

Method: 研究通过问卷调查用户创建、存储和管理密码的方式，并结合双曲贴现、现状偏差和现在偏差等行为经济学概念，分析影响密码决策的认知与行为因素，以及安全习惯是否随安全意识提高而改善。

Result: 结果表明，用户往往优先考虑密码易记和操作便利，而非长期安全性。即时成本与摩擦会促使用户拖延安全改进，并降低其采用密码管理器和多因素认证等更安全机制的意愿。

Conclusion: 仅提升安全意识不足以改变密码行为。认证政策和安全工具应降低使用门槛、减少即时操作负担，并针对用户的认知偏差进行人本化设计，从而缩小安全认知与行动之间的差距。

Abstract: Despite increasing awareness of cybersecurity risks, users continue to engage in insecure password practices, such as reusing passwords, choosing weak credentials, and neglecting security recommendations. The study explores the behavioral and cognitive factors that influence password decision-making by integrating insights from behavioral economics, particularly hyperbolic discounting, status quo bias, and present bias. We conducted a survey to analyze how people create, store and manage their passwords, examining whether security habits have improved over time in response to greater awareness. Our findings reveal that immediate convenience often outweighs long-term security considerations, leading users to prioritize memorability over strength. Additionally, we identify key psychological biases that contribute to security procrastination and resistance to adopting more secure authentication practices, such as password managers and multi-factor authentication. The study contributes to human-centered security by bridging the gap between security awareness and action, offering practical insights to design user-friendly authentication policies that align with real-world decision-making tendencies.

</details>


### [74] [Twin Agent: Context Residual Compression for Privilege Separated Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19595)
*Zhanhao Hu,Dennis Jacob,Xiao Huang,Zhaorun Chen,Bo Li,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Twin Agent双智能体架构，通过将不可信信息的探索与特权操作的执行分离，在抵御提示注入的同时尽量保持任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体会受不可信上下文中的提示注入影响，进而操纵后续推理和工具调用。现有特权分离方案虽能提升安全性，但通常损失较多效用，且需要针对任务进行复杂工程设计。

Method: Twin Agent由两个近似对称的智能体组成：Explore Agent负责检查不可信信息，Safe Agent负责执行特权操作。Explore Agent参考Safe Agent当前上下文，仅向后者传递关于下一步行动的紧凑提示，而非完整不可信内容。该设计借鉴残差编码思想，通过控制提示长度来限制信息流，并实验测量提示长度对任务效用和攻击成功率的影响。

Result: 作者在SWE-bench Lite长程软件工程任务，以及AgentDojo和DecodingTrust-Agent多工具交互任务上评估该方法。实验显示，Twin Agent能够在维持较高任务完成效用的同时阻止提示注入，并优于未防护智能体及现有特权分离基线。

Conclusion: Twin Agent提供了一种通用、低工程负担的安全设计模式：将不可信内容处理限制在探索端，再以少量行动提示支持安全端决策，可在安全性与效用之间获得更优平衡。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are vulnerable to security risks, such as prompt injection attacks from untrusted context that manipulate downstream reasoning and tool use. Existing secure-by-design approaches mitigate this risk by separating untrusted observations from privileged execution and careful control of information flow, but often degrade utility and require extensive task-specific engineering. We thus propose Twin Agent, a general privilege separation design pattern inspired by residual coding in the agent context. Twin Agent consists of two nearly symmetric agents: an Explore Agent that inspects untrusted information and a Safe Agent that executes privileged actions. The Explore Agent is conditioned on the Safe Agent's current context and communicates only compact hints to the Safe Agent about the next action to take. This design reduces the information needed to preserve task utility and thus achieves a better security--utility tradeoff, which we empirically verify by measuring how utility and attack success change as the length of hints varies. We evaluate Twin Agent on long-horizon software engineering tasks with SWE-bench Lite and on heterogeneous multi-tool interaction tasks with AgentDojo and DecodingTrust-Agent. Across both benchmarks, Twin Agent preserves high task utility while preventing prompt injection attacks, outperforming both undefended agents and privilege separation baselines.

</details>


### [75] [FedLSG: LLM-Enhanced Semantic Calibration for Federated Graph Backdoor Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19674)
*Chenyu Zhou,Yabin Peng,Wei Huang,Kunlin Li,Shuaishuai Zhang,Xinyuan Miao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出FedLSG，将大语言模型引入联邦图神经网络的后门防御，通过把图结构和客户端行为转化为文本语义，在客户端抑制触发器相关边、在服务端识别可疑更新，从而提升鲁棒性并尽量保持原始图结构完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦图神经网络容易受到后门投毒影响。现有防御多依赖预定义规则和统计特征，缺乏对图模式及客户端更新行为的语义理解，因此可能难以发现隐蔽触发器，或因过度剪除边和结构而损害正常图信息。

Method: FedLSG首先设计“图与行为到文本”的语义接地机制，将局部图拓扑、节点关联及客户端更新行为编码为自然语言描述。随后采用轻量级师生架构：服务端以完整规模的大语言模型作为教师，为聚合提供全局上下文判断并评估客户端更新的可疑性；客户端部署基于LoRA适配的学生模型，执行局部语义推理并降低疑似触发器关联边在消息传递中的影响。该框架再将语义判断与规则信号结合，用于边级消息传递调控和客户端级聚合筛选。

Result: 摘要称实验表明，FedLSG相较现有方法能够显著增强对后门攻击的抵抗能力，同时不会明显破坏图的正常结构完整性。摘要未给出具体数据集、攻击设置、评价指标、对比方法或数值结果。

Conclusion: FedLSG的核心贡献是以大语言模型提供图结构与联邦客户端行为的语义解释，使防御从纯规则驱动转向语义与规则协同驱动。其潜在价值在于同时覆盖客户端更新筛选和局部图消息传递抑制；但其实际适用性仍需结合推理开销、文本化表示质量、隐私影响及跨数据集泛化结果进一步验证。

Abstract: Federated Graph Neural Networks (FedGNNs) are highly vulnerable to backdoor poisoning, yet existing defenses typically rely on rule-based approaches that lack semantic understanding, making them vulnerable to stealthy triggers and harmful to benign structures. To solve this, we present FedLSG, the first framework that integrates large language models (LLMs) into federated graph backdoor defense. FedLSG introduces a graph and behavior to text grounding scheme that transforms local graph structures and client update behaviors into semantically rich natural language representations. The framework further adopts a lightweight student-teacher architecture. On the server side, a full scale LLM serves as a teacher, providing global contextual guidance and evaluating client updates during aggregation to identify potentially malicious participants. On the client side, a LoRA-based student is maintained to perform semantic reasoning, to suppress the influence of edges associated with backdoor triggers. By enabling semantic interpretation of both graph patterns and client behaviors, the framework adaptively incorporates rule-based signals into message passing and client aggregation for defense. Experiments demonstrate that FedLSG significantly improves resistance to backdoor attacks without compromising graph integrity.

</details>


### [76] [GhostPrompt: Cross-Image Adversarial Prompt for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19683)
*Li Zeng,Zeyu Ye,Meng Xie,Hangtao Zhang,Xianlong Wang,Yanchun Li,Zhetao Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出GhostPrompt，一种可跨图像迁移的对抗提示方法：通过一次优化得到可复用于多种图像的提示，从而稳定诱导视觉语言模型产生指定输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本的视觉语言模型对抗方法通常在优化时固定视觉输入，导致生成的提示与特定图像强绑定，跨图像泛化能力弱，攻击效率和实际适用性受限。

Method: 作者将“对抗提示的跨图像迁移性”作为研究目标。GhostPrompt采用交替式联合优化：先针对当前提示构造最困难的视觉条件，以暴露其失效场景；再更新提示，使其在这些最坏情况视觉条件下仍保持有效。该过程旨在将与具体图像无关的对抗特征蒸馏到文本提示中。

Result: 在多种主流视觉语言模型上的实验显示，GhostPrompt相较现有最佳基线方法将攻击成功率提升超过30%，同时将计算时间降低约70%。

Conclusion: GhostPrompt表明，对抗提示可以通过最坏情况驱动的联合优化获得较强的跨图像可迁移性，从而以更低计算成本实现更有效的视觉语言模型输出操控。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are known to be vulnerable to adversarial attacks, where subtle perturbations to images or texts induce erroneous outputs. However, most text-based attacks are adapted from language-model-centric methods, in which the visual input is fixed during optimization, resulting in adversarial prompts that are tied to specific images and thus limiting their attack effectiveness. To this end, we first introduce a new research perspective: cross-image transferability for adversarial prompts. We then propose GhostPrompt, an adversarial prompt that is optimized once and reused to steer VLM outputs toward attacker-specified responses across diverse images. GhostPrompt employs a joint optimization that distills image-invariant adversarial features into the prompt by "worst-case" generation. Specifically, it alternates between constructing hard visual conditions for the current prompt and updating the prompt to remain effective under these conditions. Extensive experiments on prevalent VLMs verify that \ourmethod achieves an improvement of over 30% in attack success rates compared to state-of-the-art (SoTA) baselines, while reducing computation time by ~70%. Our code is avalable at https://github.com/Ye-ze-yu/GhostPrompt.

</details>


### [77] [An Automated Framework for Extracting Reachable Attack Chains from Cyber Threat Intelligence Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19742)
*Wenbo Hou,Ning Hu,Xueping Wang,Jiahao Gu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向网络威胁情报报告的自动化攻击链抽取与可达性推理框架，将文本中的每个攻击步骤建模为“前置条件—攻击行为—后置条件”单元，并编译为类似Datalog的规则，以支持多阶段攻击路径推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTI抽取方法主要识别指标、实体或TTP标签，缺乏对攻击步骤执行条件和状态变化的建模。因此，已有结果难以进行跨步骤状态匹配、依赖连接及攻击目标可达性分析。

Method: 框架采用LLM辅助的多阶段流水线：首先抽取攻击行为骨架；随后恢复行为对应的前置条件和后置条件；将其规范化为预定义谓词；再修复步骤间断裂的依赖关系。最终，将攻击单元转换为Datalog风格规则，并通过前向推理验证攻击目标可达性、通过后向搜索生成攻击路径。

Result: 在包含20份CTI报告、334个经人工验证步骤的数据集上，该方法在攻击行为恢复的标注步骤覆盖率上优于代表性CTI抽取系统。相较端到端LLM基线，所生成的攻击单元在完整性与一致性方面更优。基于生成规则的Datalog推理在20份报告中的19份成功到达指定目标，并通过后向搜索得到34条攻击路径。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了将CTI叙事转化为带有显式状态语义的规则化攻击单元，能够有效弥补传统实体或TTP抽取的不足，并支持可执行的攻击链连通性、目标可达性及路径分析。其主要价值在于把非结构化威胁描述转化为可推理的安全知识；但摘要未说明谓词体系的泛化能力、依赖修复的错误传播情况及对不同报告风格的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) reports richly describe real-world attack processes, but their unstructured narratives cannot be directly used for automated attack-path reasoning. Existing CTI extraction methods focus on indicators, entities, or TTP labels without modeling the execution conditions and resulting states of each attack step, so the extracted knowledge supports neither state matching nor reachability analysis across multi-stage attack chains. This paper proposes an automated framework that extracts reachable attack chains by modeling each attack step as an attack unit of preconditions, an attack behavior, and postconditions. A multi-stage pipeline assisted by large language models (LLMs) extracts attack behavior skeletons, recovers their preconditions and postconditions, normalizes them into predefined predicates, and repairs broken dependencies; the resulting units are compiled into Datalog-style rules for attack-goal reachability reasoning. On a dataset of 20 CTI reports containing 334 human-validated annotated steps, our framework achieves higher annotated-step coverage than representative CTI extraction systems in recovering attack behaviors. Moreover, by explicitly generating preconditions and postconditions, it produces attack units that are more complete and consistent than those generated by end-to-end LLM baselines. On the extracted chains, Datalog inference reaches the specified attack goal in 19 of 20 reports, while backward search yields 34 attack paths under the generated rules. The source code and experimental artifacts are available in an anonymized repository. .

</details>


### [78] [DARWIN: Evolving Jailbreak Adversary and Guardrail for LLM Safety Evaluation and Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19829)
*Weiwei Qi,Zefeng Wu,Zhilin Guo,Tianhang Zheng,Chaochao Lu,Liang He,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出DARWIN，一种面向大语言模型安全的持续演化攻防框架：攻击端通过策略进化不断发现新的越狱方式，防御端通过在线对抗训练持续吸收新出现的对抗样本，以克服静态评测与固定防护数据难以应对动态攻击空间的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评测通常使用固定攻击方法，防护模型也多依赖固定恶意提示数据训练；但实际攻击者会持续调整策略、扩展攻击空间，导致静态防御容易过时。论文旨在构建能够随攻击能力共同演化的攻击评测和防御训练机制，并兼顾安全性与正常查询可用性。

Method: DARWIN-Attack将越狱建模为开放式演化过程：从广泛外部来源收集攻击策略，经由自我反思、变异、选择和基于反馈的组合生成新策略；执行阶段根据目标模型及防护系统的反馈，自适应地选择和组合已演化策略。DARWIN-Guard采用在线对抗训练，迭代使用攻击端生成的新对抗样本更新防护模型；同时联合训练恶意查询与经过伪装的正常查询，使模型侧重识别底层意图而非表层攻击模式。

Result: 摘要报告DARWIN-Attack在多个前沿模型与防护系统上取得较高攻击成功率，其中对DeepSeek-V4-Pro和YuFeng-XGuard接近100%，对GPT-5.5超过90%。DARWIN-Guard在12个安全基准上的平均不安全召回率为91.6%，优于YuFeng-XGuard和Nemotron Guard等基线；在标准正常数据集上保持接近100%的通过率。

Conclusion: 该工作主张将大模型安全从一次性的静态评测和训练转向持续演化的闭环攻防。其核心贡献是以演化攻击持续暴露新风险，并以在线对抗训练及时增强防护，从而在摘要所述实验中同时提升风险识别能力与正常任务可用性。仍需进一步验证其跨模型、跨领域长期运行时的计算成本、策略泛化性及对真实部署环境的稳定性。

Abstract: Most existing LLM safety evaluation and defense methods follow a static formulation: jailbreak vulnerabilities are evaluated with fixed attack methods, and guardrails are trained on fixed malicious prompt datasets. However, real-world adversaries continuously evolve their capabilities and expand the attack space. To address this challenge, we propose DARWIN, an evolutionary attack-defense framework that formulates jailbreaking as an open-ended evolution process and continuously updates guardrails through an evolving attack-defense loop. DARWIN-Attack is an evolutionary adversary that expands its capabilities through strategy discovery, mutation, selection, and feedback-driven composition. It collects strategies from broad external sources, generates new variants through self-reflection and genetic evolution, and retains effective strategies based on their performance against aligned LLMs. During attack execution, DARWIN-Attack adaptively selects and combines evolved strategies according to feedback from target LLMs and guardrails. Across frontier models and guardrails, it achieves state-of-the-art attack success rates, including nearly 100% on DeepSeek-V4-Pro and YuFeng-XGuard and over 90% on GPT-5.5. On the defense side, we introduce DARWIN-Guard, an online adversarial training paradigm that iteratively learns from emerging adversarial samples generated by DARWIN-Attack. To improve robustness without sacrificing utility, DARWIN-Guard jointly trains on malicious and benign disguised queries, encouraging the model to identify underlying intent rather than superficial attack patterns. DARWIN-Guard achieves an average unsafe recall of 91.6% across 12 safety benchmarks, outperforming strong guardrails such as YuFeng-XGuard and Nemotron Guard, while maintaining a nearly 100% pass rate on standard benign datasets.

</details>


### [79] [Defense Against LLM Backdoors using Critical Neuron Isolation Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19894)
*Yuxi Li,Zhibo Zhang,Kailong Wang,Xingshuo Han,Ling Shi,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出DeCNIP，一种通过识别并剪枝后门关键神经元来防御大语言模型后门攻击的方法，在极低干预比例下显著降低攻击成功率并基本保持正常能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门防御主要面向微调阶段植入的后门及简单分类任务，难以处理绕过训练流程的模型编辑型攻击，也不适配开放式文本生成。其核心不足在于依赖表层行为启发式规则，缺少对恶意激活内部表征机制的分析。

Method: DeCNIP采用统一的“发现—隔离—剪枝”流程：首先，通过优化含候选词元的有害提示与良性输入之间的交叉熵目标，搜索具有触发器特征的行为并暴露潜在后门表征；随后定位对该异常行为贡献最大的后门关键神经元；最后仅对这些神经元进行选择性剪枝，以抑制恶意影响并尽量保留原模型能力。

Result: 在6个开源大语言模型和2个基准数据集上的实验显示，DeCNIP可将攻击成功率相对降低超过95%，优于7种现有防御方法；其神经元干预比例仅为0.1%，同时可保留约97%的正常基准性能。

Conclusion: DeCNIP将后门防御从经验性行为检测推进到基于内部表征的机制化治理。结果表明，针对少量关键神经元的精准定位与剪枝能够兼顾后门消除和模型效用，但其跨模型架构、未知攻击类型及更复杂生成场景下的稳定性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where hidden triggers induce malicious outputs. Existing defenses generally fall into inference-time detection or training-time mitigation, but face two key limitations. First, they focus on fine-tuning-based backdoors (e.g., PEFT modules) and fail to address insidious model-editing attacks that bypass training pipelines. Second, they target simple classification settings and do not naturally extend to open-ended LLM generation and do not naturally extend to the open-ended generation characteristics of LLMs. Consequently, these methods focus on surface-level behavioral patterns while neglecting the deeper representational causes of malicious activations. This lack of mechanistic understanding forces defenses to depend on empirical heuristics, limiting their robustness, generality, and practical applicability in real-world LLM deployment.
  To bridge this gap, we introduce DeCNIP (Defense with Critical Neuron Isolation Pruning), which leverages representational analysis to identify and neutralize backdoors in a unified pipeline. Specifically, DeCNIP identifies trigger-like behaviors by optimizing a cross-entropy loss between harmful prompts with candidate tokens and benign inputs. This representational discovery exposes latent threats by uncovering mechanisms through which triggers hijack model weights. It then isolates Backdoor Critical Neurons (BCNs) and prunes them selectively to remove malicious influence while preserving model utility. Extensive evaluations on six open-source LLMs and two benchmark datasets demonstrate that DeCNIP achieves over 95% relative reduction in Attack Success Rate (ASR), outperforming seven state-of-the-art defenses with only 0.1% neuron intervention. Moreover, it maintains 97% of the model's performance on normal benchmarks, demonstrating its efficacy, robustness, and scalability.

</details>


### [80] [HijackKV: New Threat in Position-Independent KV Cache Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19957)
*Yichi Zhang,Zhiqi Wang,Huan Zhang,Yuchen Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文指出位置无关的KV缓存复用会引入“KV缓存劫持”风险：攻击者可使表面正常的文本块对应缓存隐式携带恶意上下文，并在后续请求复用时影响模型输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统依赖精确 token 与位置匹配的前缀缓存复用命中率较低，因此系统开始采用位置无关的KV复用。论文旨在评估这一优化是否会因缓存内容保留原始上下文信息而产生新的安全问题。

Method: 作者提出HIJACKKV框架，通过优化攻击者可控的前缀，使其后续常见、无害文本块计算得到的KV状态编码预设目标；该文本块随后作为缓存键被其他请求命中并复用。实验覆盖单次攻击、低缓存命中率、频繁重算、多轮交互及黑盒跨模型迁移等条件。

Result: 摘要报告称，HIJACKKV单次尝试的平均攻击成功率为94%；在缓存命中率仅10%、缓存有50%概率被重新计算的条件下仍有效；攻击影响可跨多轮交互持续，并可在黑盒条件下迁移到不同模型。

Conclusion: 位置无关的KV缓存复用若仅以文本匹配索引、却忽略KV状态所编码的生成上下文，会形成缓存污染与行为劫持面。安全的KV复用机制应将上下文来源、隔离边界和缓存有效性纳入设计，而不能仅依据文本块相同进行共享。

Abstract: Key-Value (KV) cache reduces inference latency in large language models (LLMs). Traditional prefix-based reuse has low cache hit rates across inference requests because it requires exact token and position matches. To improve efficiency, recent system optimizations introduce position-independent KV reuse, allowing KV cache to be reused whenever identical text chunks appear, regardless of their position in the sequence.
  We show this design introduces a new threat, KV Cache Hijacking. Since KV caches are retrieved by token match but encode the context in which they were originally computed, the KV tied to a benign-looking token chunk may encode an attacker-controlled prefix. When later reused in a victim query, this contaminated KV silently hijacks the model's behavior, even if no attacker-controlled text appears in the input.
  We introduce HIJACKKV, the first attack framework that systematically exploits this vulnerability, demonstrating its severity and practicality. HIJACKKV optimizes an attacker-controlled prefix, so that the KV computed for a subsequent common benign text encodes the attacker's goal, while the text remains unchanged for future cache hits. HIJACKKV achieves an average 94% success rate in a single attempt, remains effective under realistic constraints including low hit rates (10%) and frequent recomputation (50%), persists over multi-turn interactions, and transfers across models in black-box settings. We further provide design insights for building secure KV reuse systems.

</details>


### [81] [Taming the Security-Energy Paradox: A Green AI Approach to Optimized Android Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20003)
*Shrinidhi Sridhar,Vikas K. Malviya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文评估面向Android端恶意软件检测的MLP模型，通过INT8量化与浅层网络设计，在维持高检测精度的同时显著降低模型体积和单次推理能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动端恶意软件检测需要较强模型能力，但复杂模型会增加智能手机的电池消耗。研究旨在缓解检测安全性与设备能效之间的矛盾，支持资源受限终端上的绿色智能安全防护。

Method: 作者在TUANDROMD和DREBIN数据集上比较不同深度的多层感知机配置，包括标准FP32模型和INT8量化神经网络。评估指标涵盖分类检测准确率、模型尺寸、单次推理能耗、吞吐量，以及CPU处于高功耗状态的持续时间。

Result: INT8量化模型将模型尺寸缩小约3.5倍，单次推理能耗降至0.0189 mJ，同时检测准确率仍超过99.2%。其中，3层和4层等浅层量化网络通过提升吞吐量、缩短CPU高功耗运行时间，表现出更低的能耗成本。

Conclusion: INT8量化结合浅层MLP架构能够在Android设备上实现高精度、低能耗的本地检测。该结果表明，面向资源受限智能手机的高效安全防护具有可行性，并为移动安全领域的绿色AI设计提供了实践依据。

Abstract: An increase in advanced Android malware requires the use of deep learning models, which can run on Android devices. But there is a trade-off between security and energy use, as strong detection models can drain the battery of devices fast. This work tests different Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) model configurations to balance malware detection performance and energy efficiency. In this work, we compared standard FP32 models with optimized INT8 quantized neural networks with different model depths using TUANDROMD and DREBIN datasets for both classification performance and energy consumption. The results show that INT8 quantization reduces model size by about 3.5 times with a decrease in energy consumption to 0.0189 mJ per inference, while maintaining more than 99.2\% detection accuracy. We found that shallow quantized architectures, such as 3-layer and 4-layer QNNs, reduce energy costs by improving throughput and shortening the time of CPU operating in a high-power state. This work shows that efficient malware protection can be achieved on resource-constrained smartphones and provides a foundation for Green AI in mobile security.

</details>


### [82] [Small, Free, and Effective: Orchestrating Open-Weight Small Language Models to Outperform Single LLM for Malware Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20216)
*Adel ElZemity,Shujun Li,Budi Arief*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文评估小型开源语言模型通过多智能体编排解析恶意软件动态报告的能力，混合编排系统达到35.30%准确率，超过多数单模型基线，但仍低于具备相同证据管线的Gemini（38.22%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭源前沿模型虽擅长技术工件解读，但存在模型不透明和API成本高的问题；大型开源模型也需要较高算力与部署成本。研究旨在验证资源需求较低的小型语言模型能否借助协作编排，胜任结构化动态报告问答任务。

Method: 作者在Meta CyberSecEval Malware Analysis基准上测试了11个开源小型语言模型、3个领域预训练模型和6个前沿模型，并设计四类编排架构：分阶段证据收集与推理的多智能体流水线、双智能体对抗式辩论、通用模型与领域专家模型构成的层级咨询，以及融合证据管线与对抗推理的混合架构。

Result: Qwen3-4B与Foundation-Sec-8B组成的混合系统取得35.30%的总体准确率，高于最佳领域专用单模型基线的22.54%，也略高于最佳未接入证据管线的前沿模型基线34.77%。在使用同一证据管线时，Gemini配置达到38.22%，仍为最佳结果。

Conclusion: 证据驱动的多智能体编排能够显著提升小型语言模型对动态行为报告的解释能力，并可在较低资源约束下获得接近或超过部分前沿单模型的表现；但最强性能仍依赖更强的模型基础能力，说明编排是能力放大机制而非完全替代模型规模与质量。

Abstract: Malware analysis demands rapid interpretation of complex detonation reports spanning filesystem, network, and process behaviours. While large language models (LLMs) demonstrate impressive capabilities for technical artifact interpretation, the opacity and escalating API costs of closed-weight frontier models motivate exploration of open-weight alternatives. However, many open-weight models are large, demanding significant compute resources and incurring non-trivial hosting costs that place them beyond reach for resource-constrained deployments. This paper investigates whether orchestrated ensembles of small language models (SLMs) can match or exceed single LLM performance on structured questions about malware detonation reports. We established baselines by testing eleven open-weight SLMs, three cyber security pre-trained models, and six frontier LLMs on Meta's CyberSecEval Malware Analysis benchmark. We then designed and evaluated four orchestration architectures: (i) a multi-agent pipeline that decomposes analysis into structured evidence-collection and reasoning stages, (ii) an adversarial debate framework in which two agents iteratively critique each other's reasoning, (iii) a hierarchical consultation system that pairs a general-purpose SLM with a cyber-specialised expert model, and (iv) a hybrid architecture that combines evidence-grounded pipelines with adversarial debate reasoning. The hybrid system (Qwen3-4B with Foundation-Sec-8B) achieved 35.30% overall accuracy, exceeding the strongest cyber-specialised baseline (22.54%) and the strongest ungrounded frontier baseline (34.77%); when given the same evidence pipeline, grounded Gemini remained the strongest configuration at 38.22%. These findings show that evidence-grounded orchestration can substantially improve the performance of collaborative SLMs for supporting interpretation of malware detonation reports.

</details>


### [83] [The Ethics of Autonomous AI Agents for Offensive Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20255)
*Andreas Happe,Jürgen Cito,Jasmin Wachter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文讨论由大语言模型驱动的自主安全代理如何改变进攻性网络安全，并指出其带来的责任归属、风险治理与利益相关者影响问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统渗透测试工具通常具有确定性、用途边界清晰且依赖专业人员操作；而自主代理降低了进攻能力的使用与开发门槛，并使行为预测、事故追责和部署前安全审查更加困难。既有双重用途网络安全与人工智能伦理框架难以充分应对此类复合风险。

Method: 作者以概念分析为主，将自主安全代理的不确定性分为三个相互独立的维度：行动策略的非确定性、影响范围的开放性，以及用户规模和技能要求的不确定性。随后结合进攻与防御之间的成本不对称，分析责任如何在用户、工具开发者与第三方之间扩散，并评估利益相关者影响，据此提出分层建议。

Result: 论文认为，三类不确定性与攻防成本不对称共同促进了进攻能力的规模化。在短期内，这种技术扩散更可能使攻击方获益；长期来看，其也可能扩大防御实践的可及性。与此同时，模型行为不可解释、供应链不透明和低技能使用门槛使传统归责与治理机制失效或不足。

Conclusion: 自主安全代理并非仅是传统安全工具的自动化升级，而是引入了行动、影响和使用者层面的复合不确定性。治理重点应从单一主体责任转向覆盖使用者、开发者、模型及供应链参与方的分层责任体系，并针对不同风险层级配置审查、透明度、访问控制和事故处置机制。

Abstract: LLM-driven autonomous agents are reshaping offensive security. Unlike traditional penetration-testing tooling -- deterministic, narrowly scoped, and operated by trained practitioners -- agentic security tools exhibit \textit{indeterminacy} along three independent dimensions. First, their actions are drawn from a non-deterministic policy whose outputs resist both ex-ante and ex-post explanation, frustrating incident attribution and pre-deployment safety review. Second, their impact is open-ended due to the non-deterministic actions, agency of utilized models, and opaque LLM supply-chains. Third, their user population is indeterminate in both size and required skill: the operating skill floor for using or developing offensive capabilities has dropped sharply. These three properties are linked thematically, but are not derivable from one another. Combined with the structural cost asymmetry between offense and defense, they enable the industrialization of offensive capability. The net short-term effect favors attackers, even if the same technology may, in the long run, democratize access to defensive practice. Existing dual-use cybersecurity and AI-ethics frameworks were not designed for this combination. Our work analyzes how moral attribution becomes diffuse between users, tool-makers, and third parties when employing autonomous AI agents for offensive security. We also examine the stakeholder impact of this technology and provide stratified recommendations.

</details>


### [84] [Chained Attacks on Drone-Based Federated Learning: From Network Disruption to Device Impersonation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20280)
*Suleiman Muhammad Sabo,Hamed Alkharsh,Peilin Li,Chuadhry Mujeeb Ahmed,Aydin Abadi,Shishir Nagaraja,Rajiv Ranjan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究无人机联邦学习系统中的链式安全风险：攻击者先通过无线去认证造成节点离线，再利用凭据冒充离线节点，从而同时破坏训练可用性与身份可信性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能中的联邦学习依赖分布式终端持续参与和可靠认证。现有研究常将网络可用性与客户端认证分开讨论，本文旨在揭示两类薄弱环节被串联利用后对任务关键型无人平台训练过程的放大影响。

Method: 作者采用系统性文献综述与实验验证相结合的方法，在基于Flower框架的Raspberry Pi和Jetson两类测试平台上开展实验。实验比较IID与非IID数据分布下，节点短时离线对联邦训练的影响，并验证单因素认证场景中离线节点凭据被利用后发生身份冒充的可行性。

Result: 结果表明，无线连接的短暂中断也会引起联邦训练显著不稳定，且在非IID数据条件下影响更突出。同时，若系统仅依赖单因素认证，攻击者可在合法节点离线后使用其凭据接替参与训练，使服务器难以区分真实节点与冒充节点。

Conclusion: 论文指出，无人机联邦学习的防护不能仅关注通信连续性或认证强度中的单一方面，而应联合应对节点可用性下降与凭据冒用风险。未来防御方向包括增强抗干扰与连接恢复能力、采用多因素或持续身份认证，以及在聚合端引入节点行为与模型更新异常检测机制。

Abstract: Edge Intelligence (EI) has emerged as a transformative model for mission-critical unmanned platforms, such as drone swarms, by enabling collaborative model training at the network periphery. However, the security of FL deployments depends on both network availability and robust client authentication mechanisms. This paper investigates a chained attack against drone-based FL systems that combines network-layer denial-of-service with credential-based impersonation. We demonstrate that an adversary can: (1) force legitimate drones offline using 802.11 deauthentication attacks, and (2) subsequently impersonate the disconnected drone using extracted credentials. Through a systematic literature review and empirical validation using the Flower framework on two distinct testbeds of Raspberry Pi and Jetsons, we quantify the impact of availability disruptions under Independent and Identically Distributed (IID) and Non-Independently and Identically Distributed (Non-IID) data distributions, and confirm that single-factor authentication permits post-disconnect impersonation. Our findings reveal that even short-term wireless interruptions cascade into substantial training instability, particularly under non-IID conditions, while the authentication gap enables adversaries to seamlessly replace disconnected nodes. We discuss the compounded implications for mission-critical drone deployments and outline directions for future defenses addressing both availability and authentication vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [85] [Constant-time decoding of Gabidulin codes and their generalizations with application to RQC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20305)
*Nicolas Aragon,Chloé Baïsse,Anthony Fraga,Philippe Gaborit,Ilaria Zappatore*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出首个增强 Gabidulin（AG）码的恒定时间译码算法，并将其集成至 RQC-Block-MS-AG 方案；该实现相较原始 RQC 更快，虽较 HQC 慢约四倍，但密文与密钥尺寸约小四倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gabidulin 码是秩度量下对应 Reed-Solomon 码的重要编码结构，已用于多种高效密码系统。但此前缺少恒定时间实现，实际部署中可能因运行时间依赖输入而产生侧信道风险。因此，需要设计兼顾效率、正确性与恒定时间特性的译码方法。

Method: 作者研究在 Gabidulin 码中加入零列得到的 AG 码，并证明其包含普通 Gabidulin 码作为特例。论文构造了 AG 码的恒定时间译码流程，证明其计算复杂度为二次复杂度；同时给出 q-多项式左除法的恒定时间算法，并据此完整描述 AG 码译码过程。随后将这些算法实现并集成到 RQC-Block-MS-AG 方案中，通过基准测试评估性能。

Result: 理论上，AG 码可在二次复杂度下完成恒定时间译码。实验上，集成后的实现性能优于原始 RQC 实现；与 HQC 相比，运行速度仍约慢四倍，但其密文大小和密钥大小约缩小四倍。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了 Gabidulin 及 AG 码恒定时间译码实现的空白，为相关密码方案的安全工程化部署提供了关键基础。其结果表明，RQC-Block-MS-AG 在计算速度与通信、存储开销之间提供了具有吸引力的折中，尤其适合重视密钥和密文紧凑性的应用场景。

Abstract: Gabidulin codes are a rank metric analog of Reed-Solomon codes. Although these codes are used in different very efficient rank-based cryptosystems like the RQC cryptosystem or the Loidreau cryptosystem, there was no constant-time implementation of Gabidulin codes, when having a constant-time implementation is crucial for real-life development of cryptosystems. In this paper, we propose the first constant-time decoding algorithm of Augmented Gabidulin (AG) codes, a simple variation on Gabidulin codes where one adds zero columns to Gabidulin codes, and which contains the case of Gabidulin codes. These AG codes are used in practice in the most efficient variations of the RQC cryptosystem. We prove that AG code decoding can be achieved with quadratic complexity. We further present a constant-time algorithm for the left division of $q$-polynomials along with a complete description of the AG code decoding procedure. These algorithms are integrated into the RQC-Block-MS-AG scheme, and we evaluate the performance of our implementation through benchmarks. Our results show that our implementation outperforms the original RQC, though it remains approximately four times slower than HQC. However, it achieves ciphertexts and key sizes about four times smaller, highlighting an appealing trade-off between performance and compactness.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Generalized Constraint Projection: Four-Dimensional Type Inference for Dynamic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19693)
*Qunhui Zhang*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出广义约束投影（GCP），用于动态类型语言的零注解类型推断；其核心是在定义期分离保存四类参数类型证据，并在每次调用时创建独立投影会话，以避免传统统一约束系统中的伪冲突与重复注解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态语言的函数参数类型同时受函数内部赋值、显式声明、调用上下文需求及结构化操作影响。若将这些证据合并为单一约束集，容易产生不必要的类型冲突，或迫使开发者补充冗余类型注解。因此，需要一种既能保留不同证据来源语义、又能支持调用时特化和高阶函数场景的推断机制。

Method: GCP将四类证据存储于稳定的定义期模板中的独立单调槽位。普通调用在新的投影会话中检查具体实参并特化返回类型，但不修改原模板；柯里化则产生剩余的投影函数。系统采用轮廓等式匹配（OEM）作为结构兼容预序，并通过开放的双向委托协议处理结构关系；此外，以“future this”机制保留接收者类型信息，支持链式接口中的子类型细化。理论分析基于有限高度类型预序及公平单调迭代，并对无递归核心语言Outline0给出运行时性质证明。

Result: 在严格成功片段中，论文证明了GCP的单调性、局部与全局收敛性，并将有效更新次数界定为O(Nh_T)；还证明了条件性的投影义务健全性、投影终止性、多模块收敛性，以及公平单调迭代下的顺序无关性。对于纯粹、无递归的Outline0，进一步证明大步求值的定义性、类型保持、运行时接收者保留及投影与求值的一致性。作者还将该方法实现于Outline动态语言，并用于从无注解Python代码恢复PEP 484类型注解，以服务后续编译。

Conclusion: GCP通过“定义期证据分离、调用期独立投影”的架构，为动态语言类型推断提供了比统一约束求解更细粒度的处理方式。其形式化结果表明，该框架在受限类型结构下具备可终止、可收敛且顺序稳定的推断行为，并能覆盖柯里化、结构类型兼容和保留接收者信息的流式接口等实践需求。

Abstract: Type inference for dynamically typed languages must reconcile four distinct sources of evidence for function parameters: internal assignments, explicit declarations, contextual requirements, and structural operations. Existing systems often merge these sources into one constraint set, causing spurious conflicts or requiring redundant annotations. We present Generalized Constraint Projection (GCP), a zero-annotation inference framework that stores the four sources in separate monotone slots on a stable definition-time template and checks each call in a fresh projection session. Ordinary calls verify concrete arguments and specialize return types without modifying the template, while currying produces residual projected functions. GCP uses Outline Equational Matching (OEM), a structural compatibility preorder with an open bidirectional delegation protocol, and future this, a receiver-preserving extension for subtype-refining fluent APIs. On the strict success fragment of a finite-height type preorder, we prove monotonicity, local and global convergence in $O(Nh_T)$ effective updates, conditional projection-obligation soundness, projection termination, multi-module convergence, and order independence under fair monotone iteration. For the pure, recursion-free core Outline0, we additionally prove big-step evaluation definedness, type preservation, runtime receiver retention, and projection-evaluation coherence. We instantiate GCP in the Outline dynamic language as a typed substrate for ontology worlds and apply it to unannotated Python source to recover PEP 484 annotations for downstream compilation.

</details>


### [87] [Towards Relating Ciao Assertions and LPTP Theorems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20249)
*Marco Pérez,Pedro López-García,Jose F. Morales,Manuel V. Hermenegildo,Fred Mesnard*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文研究将 Ciao Prolog 的断言规范转换为 LPTP 一阶逻辑公式，以连接抽象解释驱动的断言验证与演绎式定理证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ciao 的断言检查擅长描述程序、谓词及执行状态属性，而 LPTP 提供形式化的逻辑表达与证明机制。两者缺乏系统对应关系，限制了分析验证与定理证明能力的联合使用。

Method: 作者提出系统化翻译方案，将 Ciao 断言映射为 LPTP 公式；按逻辑可编码性对断言类别进行刻画；针对无法直接翻译的断言，设计近似策略与辅助逻辑构造；并分析不同转换方案的健全性和完备性权衡。

Result: 研究建立了断言式规范与逻辑式规范之间的部分对应关系，明确了可直接编码的断言范围，并为不可编码情形提供可控的近似表达路径。

Conclusion: 该方案有望将 Ciao 的断言检查与 LPTP 的演绎验证紧密集成，从而结合抽象解释的自动分析能力和逻辑定理证明的形式化证明能力。

Abstract: Abstract interpretation-based verification is a central component of the Ciao Prolog system, enabling expressive specifications of properties of programs, predicates, and execution states. Independently, the LPTP (Logic Programming Theorem Proving) framework offers a first-order logical formalism for expressing and proving properties of predicates.  In this paper, we address a fundamental issue in relating these two frameworks: studying the translation of Ciao assertions into LPTP formulae and  identifying a partial correspondence between assertion-based and logic-based specifications. We introduce a systematic translation scheme, characterize assertion classes according to their logical encodability, and propose approximation strategies and auxiliary constructs for non-translatable cases, and finally analyze the resulting soundness and completeness trade-offs. We  argue that our proposal enables a tight integration of Ciao's assertion checking with LPTP-based deductive verification, thereby leveraging their complementary capabilities.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [Inference-Behaviour Semantics for All$^\ast$ Connectives in Two-Dimensional Sequent Calculi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19419)
*Sophie Nagler*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文系统检验了“推理行为语义学”（I-bS）在二维序列演算中对逻辑联结词的刻画能力：在10,816种候选规则对中，恰有21种对应具有明确语义的联结词，并据此描绘了多类逻辑系统中联结词之间的语义关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统证明论语义学主张表达式的意义由其在推理中的使用及其操作规则决定，但这一思想需要一种可枚举、可验证且能区分有意义规则与无意义规则的形式化方法。论文旨在验证I-bS是否能够系统地从联结词规则中恢复其语义，并统一比较不同逻辑中的联结词。

Method: 作者以次结构上最小的可推导关系为基准，通过考察联结词在其可定义性证明中的句法使用，衡量给定规则对是否满足I-bS的定义性条件。随后穷举二维序列演算中至多含两个前提序列、至多含两个活跃公式的全部10,816个联结词规则对，并为通过检验的规则对确定其对应的最小次结构语义子句。

Result: 穷举结果表明，仅21个规则对定义了有意义的联结词。这些联结词包括底、顶、直觉主义否定与对偶直觉主义否定、群与格意义下的合取、析取和蕴涵，以及它们的逆与对偶形式。基于这些结果，论文建立了联结词在资源敏感逻辑、经典逻辑、直觉主义逻辑、对偶直觉主义逻辑、极小逻辑和格逻辑之间的精确语义关联图。尤其重要的是，直觉主义否定、析取和蕴涵分别只刻画了相应经典联结词意义的一半。

Conclusion: 论文为I-bS提供了大规模穷举验证，表明该框架能以最小次结构规则对为语义子句，可靠识别并分类联结词。其核心贡献不仅是得到21个可定义联结词的完整清单，更在于揭示不同逻辑中联结词并非简单同名对应：直觉主义联结词与经典联结词之间存在可精确描述的部分语义对应关系。

Abstract: Inference-behaviour semantics (I-bS) is a new approach to proof-theoretic semantics, grounded in two inferentialist principles: (1) the use of an expression in reasoning determines its meaning, and (2) a connective is defined by its operational rules. I-bS operationalises these ideas by measuring the syntactic use of a connective in the proof of its definability, against a substructurally minimal derivability relation. I-bS thereby gives the meaning of a connective in terms of its semantic clause, i.e. minimal substructural rule pair.
  This paper validates and verifies I-bS by applying it to all $10,816$ connective rule pairs that can be formulated in two-dimensional sequent calculi using at most two premiss sequents and at most two active formulae. As a result, we find semantic clauses for exactly $21$ meaningful connectives, namely bottom, top, two negations (intuitionistic and dual-intuitionistic), group and lattice conjunction, disjunction and implication, as well as their converses and inverses.
  We use these results to precisely map the semantic interrelations among the connectives, across linear, classical, intuitionistic, dual-intuitionistic, minimal, and lattice logic. Most notably, we find that intuitionistic negation, disjunction and implication each capture half of the meaning of their classical counterparts.

</details>


### [89] [Information Propagation and Contraction in Functional Interpretations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19723)
*Chuangjie Xu*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出“信息核”框架，将函数解释拆分为仿射信息传播与收缩两部分，并统一刻画Herbrand式与Dialectica式解释及其可扩展的辅助信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有函数解释通常难以模块化地说明提取出的实现子携带何种额外信息，以及重复假设导致的收缩如何处理。论文旨在以统一代数接口分离并解决这两个问题。

Method: 作者定义信息核，用于规定有限类型对象关联的信息、精确对象与信息的相容性，以及信息沿函数的传播规则。由任意信息核构造公式翻译，并证明仿射有限类型算术的可靠性。为处理允许收缩的有限类型算术，进一步引入按公式索引的收缩结构，将重复假设产生的多个挑战归约为单一挑战。

Result: 有限候选集合及其并运算导出Herbrand风格解释；精确信息结合挑战选择导出通常的Dialectica解释，后者建立在原子公式可判定的受限算术系统上。该框架同时给出了相应解释的统一可靠性路径。

Conclusion: 信息核为函数解释提供了模块化、可组合的规范机制，可系统地为既有解释加入连续性等辅助数据，并清晰地区分信息传播与收缩处理这两类核心结构。

Abstract: This paper separates two components of functional interpretations: affine information propagation and contraction. We introduce information nuclei as an algebraic interface to capture the affine component. An information nucleus specifies what information is associated with finite-type objects, how exact objects are compatible with such information, and how information is propagated through functions. From any information nucleus we obtain a formula translation and a soundness theorem for affine finite-type arithmetic. Extending soundness to finite-type arithmetic with contraction requires one additional ingredient: a formula-indexed contraction structure reducing the challenges generated by duplicated assumptions to a single challenge. Finite collections of candidates with union yield a Herbrand-style interpretation, while exact information with challenge selection yields the usual Dialectica interpretation over an arithmetic system restricted to decidable primitive formulas. The resulting framework provides a uniform method for specifying the information carried by extracted realizers, allowing existing functional interpretations to be systematically enriched with auxiliary data, such as continuity information.

</details>


### [90] [Neuro-Symbolic AI for Korean Criminal Law: Sentencing Prediction and Document Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19740)
*Yeonseok Lee*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: The Korean criminal justice system utilizes summary proceedings (guyaksik) to expedite high-volume minor infractions, such as simple driving under the influence (DUI), unlicensed driving, and minor traffic casualties. Although this mechanism improves judicial throughput, processing these cases creates a substantial administrative burden for prosecutors, driving the need for automated systems that can precisely translate unstructured legal text into deterministic statutory outcomes. While recent Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at semantic extraction, their probabilistic nature inherently limits their reliability in Legal Judgment Prediction tasks. Specifically, when confronted with the arithmetic constraints of legal statutes, LLMs can produce hallucinations. Given that legal accountability permits virtually no tolerance for stochastic errors, purely neural architectures remain limited in their direct judicial applications. To address these limitations, we propose a Neuro-Symbolic framework that bridges unstructured legal facts with formal verification. Our architecture restricts the LLM exclusively to semantic extraction, while offloading statutory fine calculations to a Satisfiability Modulo Theories solver. This division of labor reduces hallucination risks during computation. Furthermore, we incorporate a Human-in-the-Loop verification scheme to preserve professional legal oversight. We formalize the 2026 Sentencing Guidelines for Traffic Offenses within this pipeline, demonstrating a deterministic approach to supporting summary indictments.

</details>


### [91] [The Dynamic Turn in Paraconsistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19906)
*Rafael Ongaratto,Hans van Ditmarsch*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出一组用于处理暂时性矛盾的动态认知旁一致逻辑，包括动作模型逻辑AMLFI1、公开宣布逻辑PALFI1及含事实变化的UMLFI1，并证明其可靠性与完全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动态认知逻辑通常难以在不导致逻辑爆炸的情况下表达矛盾信息的获得、传播与消解。作者旨在将动态更新机制引入旁一致逻辑LFI1，以刻画主体认知状态中暂时存在的矛盾。

Method: 以旁一致逻辑LFI1为基础构造动作模型扩展AMLFI1；将其限制为公开宣布情形得到PALFI1，并论证其与已有旁一致公开宣布逻辑的公理系统彼此可容许；进一步通过加入事实变化机制构造UMLFI1。各系统均建立在KLFI1、KB4LFI1和S5LFI1等认知旁一致逻辑之上，并给出可靠性和完全性证明。

Result: AMLFI1、PALFI1和UMLFI1均获得了形式化语义与相应的可靠性、完全性结果。PALFI1与近期提出的另一公开宣布旁一致逻辑在证明能力上等价，尽管二者采用不同公理化方式。

Conclusion: 该工作为动态认知旁一致推理提供了统一框架，使系统能够形式化表示矛盾信息的引入及其后续解决，并将动作更新、公开宣布和事实变化纳入同一旁一致逻辑体系。

Abstract: In this work we propose a dynamic turn in paraconsistency. We introduce AMLFI1, the action model extension of the paraconsistent logic LFI1. A special case is PALFI1, a paraconsistent logic of public announcements. It corresponds to another, recently published, paraconsistent public announcement logic: the differences in their axiomatizations are mutually admissible. We also introduce UMLFI1, that extends AMLFI1 with factual change. Soundness and completeness are proven for all logics, and all extend the epistemic paraconsistent logics KLFI1, KB4LFI1 and S5LFI1, known from the literature. With such dynamic epistemic paraconsistent logics we can formalize obtaining and resolving provisional contradictions.

</details>


### [92] [A SAT-Based Exact Approach for Radio k-Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19997)
*Huong Vu Thanh,Duc Dao Van,Khanh To Van*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于无线k标号问题的精确增量SAT求解框架，通过紧凑顺序编码和子句复用逐步收紧标号跨度，在146个基准实例上显著提升了已知结果与最优性认证数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线k标号要求满足依赖图距离的标签差约束，目标是最小化最大标签跨度。已有构造法、启发式算法及通用优化模型通常难以在广泛图类上同时获得高质量解和可验证的最优性证明，因此需要更有效的精确优化方法。

Method: 方法采用SAT编码表示顶点整数标签及其距离约束，并以候选跨度为界进行可满足性判定。框架从较宽的跨度界开始或逐步收紧可行跨度，使用增量SAT机制保留前序调用中学习到的子句，避免每次测试新跨度时重建完整公式。研究还将SAT结果与由CPLEX、Gurobi求解的ILP模型结合，以增强可行解搜索及最优性认证能力。

Result: 在来自九类图族的146个基准实例中，该框架取得38个新的最佳已知无线数，并在130个实例上达到或改进既有最佳结果。与CPLEX、Gurobi等商业优化求解器相比，SAT方法在整体解质量上更优，也明显优于既有启发式方法。结合SAT与ILP后，109个实例获得了经认证的最优解。

Conclusion: 增量SAT求解能够作为无线k标号这类困难图标号优化问题的实用精确框架。其关键优势在于紧凑编码、跨跨度测试的学习子句复用，以及与ILP模型的互补协作；实验表明该方案不仅提高了解质量，也大幅扩展了具有严格最优性证明的基准实例范围。

Abstract: The radio $k$-labeling (or $k$-coloring) problem seeks a minimum-span assignment of nonnegative integer labels to the vertices of a connected graph $G$ such that $ |f(u)-f(v)| \ge k+1-d(u,v) $ for all vertex pairs. Although numerous theoretical constructions and some heuristic algorithms have been proposed, existing approaches generally fail to provide certified optimal solutions for broad graph classes. This paper presents an exact SAT-based framework for radio k-labeling that combines a compact order encoding with incremental SAT solving. The proposed framework incrementally tightens the admissible span while reusing learned clauses across SAT calls, avoiding repeated formula reconstruction. Experimental results on 146 benchmark instances from nine graph families demonstrate that the proposed approach establishes 38 new best-known radio numbers while matching or improving the best-known radio numbers on 130 of the 146 benchmark instances. The proposed SAT framework outperforms state-of-the-art commercial optimization solvers, including CPLEX and Gurobi, in terms of overall solution quality, and substantially improves upon previously published heuristic methods. Furthermore, by combining the SAT frameworks with ILP models solved by CPLEX and Gurobi, the study certifies optimal solutions for 109 of the 146 benchmark instances, substantially expanding the set of radio-labeling benchmarks with proven optimality. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of incremental SAT solving as a practical exact optimization framework for difficult graph-labeling problems.

</details>


### [93] [What is a Model of the Linear Lambda Calculus?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20088)
*Arturo De Faveri*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文从代数视角系统刻画线性λ演算的模型，并证明三种看似不同的模型化框架彼此等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性λ演算要求变量恰好使用一次，传统λ演算的模型理论不能直接适用。作者旨在澄清何种代数结构能够恰当地作为线性λ演算的语义模型，并统一已有的互补观点。

Method: 以线性λ项构成的算子为起点，将其代数定义为候选模型；随后证明该定义与线性Curry λ-代数、带内部抽象操作的半闭算子三者等价。论文还利用线性组合子B、C、I给出有限等式公理化，并借助半闭算子与预层范畴中的幺半闭结构之间的联系建立表示结果。

Result: 作者证明了三类结构的等价性：线性λ项算子代数、线性λ-代数和半闭算子。进一步给出了基于B、C、I组合子的线性λ-代数有限等式表示，并表明每个此类模型都可表示为某个自然预层幺半闭范畴中的自反对象。

Conclusion: 该工作为线性λ演算提供了统一且可操作的代数语义基础：不同的语法、组合子和范畴论表述刻画同一类模型；同时，其表示定理将抽象代数模型与幺半闭范畴中的自反对象联系起来。

Abstract: We investigate the notion of model of the linear $λ$-calculus from an algebraic perspective. Our starting point is the operad of linear $λ$-terms, whose algebras provide a natural candidate. We prove that this notion of model is equivalent to two other structures: a linear analogue of Curry's $λ$-algebras, and semiclosed operads, a class of operads equipped with an internal abstraction operation. The equivalence between these three approaches unifies three complementary answers to the question of what should be regarded as a model of the linear $λ$-calculus. As a second contribution, we give a finite equational presentation for the linear variant of $λ$-algebras using the linear combinators $\mathbf{B}$, $\mathbf{C}$, and $\mathbf{I}$. Finally, exploiting the equivalence with semiclosed operads, we establish a linear analogue of Scott's representation theorem by showing that every model arises as a reflexive object in a natural monoidal closed category of presheaves.

</details>


### [94] [Linearising Explicit Substitutions using Intersection Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20179)
*Ana Jorge Almeida,Sandra Alves,Mário Florido*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向显式替换演算的新术语展开方法，用于建立显式替换λ演算与Boudol带重数的资源感知λ演算之间的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有术语展开最初用于关联交叉类型系统中的项与线性项，随后被扩展到其他次结构类型系统及定量类型的研究。本文旨在将这一技术推广到显式替换场景，以刻画函数参数具有有限可用性时的资源使用语义。

Method: 作者定义一种适用于显式替换演算的术语展开。该展开将含有显式替换的λ项映射或关联到Boudol的带重数资源λ演算中的项，其中重数用于表示函数参数可被使用的次数或可用资源量。

Result: 该方法建立了显式替换λ演算与带重数资源感知λ演算之间的形式化联系，使前者中的替换结构能够以资源数量受限的参数使用方式表达。

Conclusion: 新术语展开为研究显式替换、资源控制与次结构类型之间的关系提供了统一工具，并有望支持对资源敏感计算模型及其类型系统性质的进一步分析。

Abstract: Term expansion was originally introduced in 2004 as a way to relate terms typed in an intersection type system with linear terms. Recently, new applications of term expansion include the relation of lambda-terms with terms typed in other substructural type systems, such as the relevant and the ordered type systems, and the use of quantitative types to relate the strongly normalising lambda-terms with weak linear terms that share the same normal form. Here we define a new term expansion for a calculus with explicit substitutions, using it to relate a lambda-calculus with explicit substitutions to Boudol's resource aware lambda-calculus with multiplicities, where function arguments have a possibly limited availability.

</details>


### [95] [Quantum Term Rewrite Systems: Applications to Complexity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20170)
*Kostia Chardonnet,Emmanuel Hainry,Romain Péchoux,Thomas Vinet*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出量子项重写系统（QTRS），将经典项重写系统扩展到量子计算，用于在保留复杂度可认证性的同时描述量子计算过程，并刻画量子多项式时间可计算函数类FBQP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典项重写系统适合进行终止性与复杂度等静态分析，但无法直接表达量子计算。论文旨在构建兼具量子计算能力、物理可实现性及自动化复杂度分析潜力的重写模型。

Method: 作者定义QTRS及其满足物理可实现性的约束，改造经典终止性证明和归约长度上界分析技术；进一步界定一类可终止的QTRS，并将其编译为统一量子电路族，电路规模由重写归约长度控制。同时证明该类系统对量子电路具有通用表达能力。

Result: 对于所定义的终止QTRS类别，任意计算可编译为统一量子电路族，且电路大小不超过对应的归约长度；反向上，该类别能够模拟量子电路。因此，论文得到对FBQP的计算模型刻画。

Conclusion: QTRS将量子计算与基于重写的静态验证结合起来，既可表达通用量子计算，又支持终止性和复杂度界的认证，为可验证量子程序及量子复杂度分析提供了新的形式化基础。

Abstract: Term Rewrite Systems (TRS) is a computational model offering a level of abstraction well-suited towards static analysis, e.g., termination or complexity analyses. In this paper, we introduce Quantum Term Rewrite Systems (QTRS), an extension of TRS to quantum computing, thus allowing to benefit from quantum advantage while being able to certify the complexity. We ensure that QTRS correspond to physically realizable processes and adapt techniques to obtain termination certificates or generic bounds on the reduction length. We delineate a class of terminating QTRS that can be compiled to uniform families of quantum circuits of size bounded by the reduction length. Conversely, this class is universal for quantum circuits. In particular, we show a characterization of the class of functions computable in quantum polynomial time, known as $\mathtt{FBQP}$.

</details>


### [96] [A Logical 3-valued Semantics for Nondeterministic Choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20178)
*Alessandro Aldini,Pierluigi Graziani,Claudio Antares Mezzina,Gandolfo Vergottini*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于反应式非确定性系统中计算错误建模的三值对称非确定性析取联结词，并以非确定性矩阵为语义框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顺序求值逻辑会在错误与正常值混合时引入残余非对称性，难以忠实刻画并发计算中的非确定性选择与错误传播。

Method: 作者结合Kleene的容错语义与Bochvar的对称错误持久性，以最小化方式构造新的三值析取；该联结词在非确定性矩阵中定义，使特定输入组合可产生多个合法语义结果。

Result: 所得到的语义能够在含错误的混合情形中给出真正的非确定性输出，同时保持析取运算的交换性和操作层面的对称性。

Conclusion: 该工作为并发及反应式计算中的错误与非确定性选择提供了更贴合操作行为的逻辑形式化工具，并克服了基于顺序求值语义的析取逻辑的方向性偏差。

Abstract: We propose a logical formalisation of computational errors in reactive, nondeterministic systems. To this aim, we introduce a new three-valued symmetric nondeterministic disjunction, designed to provide a faithful logical representation of the nondeterministic choice arising in concurrent computations. The connective is defined within the framework of nondeterministic matrices (Nmatrices) and derives from a minimal combination of Kleene's tolerant semantics and Bochvar's symmetric error persistence, thereby eliminating the residual asymmetry induced by sequential evaluation strategies such as McCarthy's logic. The resulting semantics admits genuinely nondeterministic outcomes in mixed cases involving errors, while preserving commutativity and operational symmetry.

</details>


### [97] [Dynamic Logic with Parallel Operator for Verifying Communication Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20180)
*Luiz C. F. Fernandez,Mario R. F. Benevides*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种带并行算子的动态逻辑，用于形式化验证密码协议的真实性与安全性；该逻辑融合通信动作和Dolev-Yao入侵者模型，并给出完备公理化及终止、可靠、完备的表演算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码协议通常在存在攻击者干预的通信环境中运行，需同时刻画协议参与方的并发行为、通信过程及攻击者能力。已有动态逻辑虽可描述程序行为，但对密码协议中的敌手推理与并行执行支持不足。

Method: 作者在既有带并行算子的动态逻辑基础上扩展语法与语义，引入源自Dolev-Yao模型的攻击者知识和操作能力，并将通信动作纳入逻辑表达。随后利用底层逻辑的完全可公理化性质构造扩展系统的完备公理体系；此外设计对应的表演算，并分别证明其终止性、可靠性和完备性。

Result: 扩展后的逻辑能够表达和验证密码协议执行中的真实性与安全性性质，尤其适用于具有并发交互和敌手参与的场景。论文理论上获得了完整的公理化系统，并建立了一个具备终止性、可靠性及完备性的自动化推理基础。

Conclusion: 该工作将并发动态逻辑与符号化攻击者模型结合，为密码协议的形式化验证提供了具有严格元理论保证的逻辑框架。其主要贡献在于统一支持通信、并行和攻击者行为，并通过公理系统与表演算保证推理的正确性和表达完整性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a dynamic logic with parallel operators for the formal verification of authenticity and safety properties of cryptographic protocols. The logic incorporates communication actions and is specifically designed to reason about protocol executions in adversarial environments. We extend an existing dynamic logic with parallel operators by introducing concepts derived from the Dolev-Yao intruder model. As the underlying logic is completely axiomatizable, we obtain a complete axiomatization for the extended system. Furthermore, we develop a tableau calculus for the proposed logic and prove its termination, soundness, and completeness.

</details>


### [98] [A Typing System for the Linear Lambda-Calculus in de Bruijn Notation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20181)
*Philippe de Groote,Vincent Tourneur*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种适用于 de Bruijn 表示法下线性 λ 演算的类型系统，通过类型规则直接保证良类型项满足线性使用约束，并证明了主语归约性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线性 λ 演算中，变量必须恰好使用一次。传统方法通常需要额外进行变量出现次数检查；而在 de Bruijn 表示中，变量由索引表示，使这类检查及资源管理更复杂。论文旨在设计一种能将线性约束内嵌于类型推导过程的系统。

Method: 作者构造了一种受 Hodas 与 Miller 资源消耗模型启发的类型纪律。该系统通过类型环境和推导规则表达变量资源的消费情况，使变量的线性使用由类型检查自动确保，而非在项上单独执行出现次数验证。随后针对归约规则证明类型保持。

Result: 该类型系统保证所有可类型化的 de Bruijn 线性 λ 项都是线性的，即每个受约束变量符合线性使用要求。论文还建立了主语归约性质：若一个项可类型化且发生归约，则归约后的项仍具有相应的类型。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，资源敏感的类型系统能够有效处理 de Bruijn 记法中的线性约束，并免除显式出现次数检查。主语归约证明进一步确认了该系统与计算过程的类型安全兼容性。

Abstract: We introduce a typing system that is particularly well suited for typing the linear lambda-calculus in de Bruijn notation. This typing discipline, which is reminiscent of Hodas' and Miller's model of resource consumption, guarantees that any well-typed term is linear without the need for an occurrence check. We then establish the subject reduction property.

</details>


### [99] [Basic Model Theory for Path Predicate Modal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20183)
*Raul Fervari,Santiago Figueira,Gabriel Goren-Roig,Leonardo Torres*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文研究路径谓词模态逻辑（PPML）的模型论基础，重点讨论其表达能力、Hennessy-Milner 类的定义方式，以及类似 van Benthem 定理的刻画结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPML以关系谓词替代传统模态逻辑中的命题原子，可作为 XPath、DataGL 等数据感知形式化语言的抽象基础。为理解这类语言能够表达和不能表达什么，需要建立相应的模型论刻画。

Method: 作者考察PPML中Hennessy-Milner类的多种定义方案，并尝试建立van Benthem式刻画定理；分析过程中重点处理关系谓词带来的新语义特征，同时将其与标准模态逻辑中的双模拟和一阶可定义性方法进行比较。

Result: 摘要表明，论文系统讨论了PPML适用的Hennessy-Milner框架及其van Benthem式表达能力刻画，并识别出相较于普通模态逻辑，处理路径和关系谓词时面临的关键技术挑战与可复用的标准方法。摘要未给出具体定理陈述、充分必要条件或证明细节。

Conclusion: 该工作为PPML及相关数据感知查询语言提供了模型论视角下的表达能力分析基础，说明经典模态逻辑的刻画工具可以在适当调整后用于关系谓词驱动的路径逻辑。

Abstract: Path Predicate Modal Logic (PPML) is a generalization of Basic Modal Logic, where atoms are relational predicates instead of propositional symbols. The study of PPML is motivated as a way to abstractly investigate data-aware formalisms, such as XPath or DataGL. In this paper, we investigate some basic model theoretical aspects of PPML to better characterize its expressive power. More concretely, we investigate different ways of defining Hennessy-Milner classes, and a van Benthem characterization theorem. In doing so, we discuss the main challenges of dealing with the novel features of PPML, and what are the similarities with the standard approaches.

</details>


### [100] [MaudeTypedLog: A Typed Interpreter for Prolog in Maude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20184)
*Enrique Gallifa-Tronch,João Barbosa,Santiago Escobar*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出MaudeTypedLog：基于Maude实现的带类型统一算法与Prolog解释器，可依据Typed SLD-resolution在运行时检测程序和查询中的类型错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Prolog通常被视为无类型逻辑编程语言，但部分查询会产生类型错误。既有为Prolog引入静态类型系统的尝试未被广泛采用，因此需要一种能够兼顾Prolog执行模型并有效识别类型问题的方法。

Method: 作者使用Maude实现带类型的统一算法，并以其为核心构建MaudeTypedLog解释器。该解释器遵循逻辑编程的Typed SLD-resolution操作语义，在归结和统一过程中传播及检查类型约束。

Result: 所实现的解释器能够动态发现两类类型错误：程序本身包含的类型不一致，以及用户查询导致的类型不匹配。该结果表明类型化统一可被整合到Prolog式解释执行中。

Conclusion: MaudeTypedLog提供了一种以操作语义为基础的动态类型错误检测方案，避免完全依赖难以普及的静态类型扩展，并为更可靠的Prolog程序执行提供了实现路径。

Abstract: Prolog is traditionally thought of as an untyped logic programming language, although there are queries that result in a type error. Several attempts of statically introducing a type discipline in Prolog have been made but they have not been widely adopted. We use Maude to implement a typed unification algorithm and use it as the basis for an interpreter for Prolog called MaudeTypedLog. This interpreter follows the Typed SLD-resolution operational semantics for logic programming, that makes it possible to detect type errors in both programs and queries dynamically.

</details>


### [101] [Robust Classification in ML: A Topological Semantics Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20185)
*Dominik Pichler,Mirko Tagliaferri*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出一个基于模态逻辑拓扑语义的鲁棒分类形式化框架，将分类鲁棒性定义为分类结论在某个非空开邻域内持续为真，并给出相应逻辑的公理系统与模型构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒分类通常从输入微小扰动下预测是否稳定的角度理解，但缺少能够统一表达鲁棒区域、分类条件及其逻辑关系的形式化语言。作者旨在以几何和逻辑方式刻画鲁棒性，使鲁棒分类的分析与解释可被严格推理。

Method: 作者以特征向量作为模型中的评价点，以公式表示显式分类；在S4拓扑空间中引入鲁棒性模态算子，将公式在一点的鲁棒成立解释为其在该点某个非空开邻域中恒成立。进一步定义鲁棒性敏感条件联结词：当见证前件鲁棒性的邻域包含于后件真值集合时，该条件在该点成立。论文随后证明该逻辑的可靠性与完全性，并提出最小鲁棒模型，以指定的鲁棒性约束构造对应模型。

Result: 该框架能够表达鲁棒分类区域与其他分类属性之间的全局包含关系，并提供了完备的公理化推理体系。最小鲁棒模型方法则为从约束出发生成和研究分类模型提供了构造性工具。

Conclusion: 论文将鲁棒分类转化为拓扑模态逻辑中的局部真值持久性问题，建立了可验证、可推理且可构造的理论基础。其主要价值在于为鲁棒分类行为的形式化分析、解释和结构化描述提供统一语言，但摘要未说明该方法在具体数据集、学习算法或计算复杂度方面的实证表现。

Abstract: Robust classification is commonly understood as the stability of a classifier under small perturbations (often adversarial) of input data. In this paper, we propose a logical framework for robust classification grounded in topological semantics for modal logic. Evaluation points are feature vectors representing machine-readable objects, and formulas express explicit classifications. Robustness is interpreted geometrically as local truth persistence: a classification is robust at a point if it holds throughout some non-empty open neighbourhood of that point. Building on this perspective, we introduce a logical language with a robustness modality interpreted over S4 topological spaces, together with a robustness-sensitive conditional connective. This conditional connective captures global inclusion relations between robust regions and other properties of the classifier: it holds at a point when the neighbourhood witnessing the robustness of one formula is contained in the truth set of another. In this way, robust classifications can be systematically linked to classification conditions. We provide a sound and complete axiomatisation of the resulting logic. Finally, we introduce Minimal Robust Models, a constructive method for generating models from specified robustness constraints, which yields formal tools for analysing, explaining, and structuring robust classification behaviour.

</details>


### [102] [From Dag-Like Proofs to Boolean Circuits in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20186)
*Lorenzo Saraiva,Edward Hermann Haeusler*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出将蕴涵型极小逻辑中自然演绎证明经水平压缩后形成的类有向无环图可推导性结构（DLDS）编码为布尔电路的方法，并通过Lean验证其局部语义正确性及受限树形片段上的电路接受性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然演绎树证明通常包含大量重复子推导，水平压缩可降低冗余但会引入图结构中的依赖传播与假设解除处理难题。将DLDS转化为布尔电路，旨在获得可计算、可验证的证明正确性判定机制，并支持自动定理证明与形式化认证。

Method: 作者形式化定义了从DLDS到布尔电路的构造。该电路针对给定路径赋值，编码证明图中的路径选择、依赖传播以及假设解除条件；论文证明逐点正确性，即任意固定路径赋值下，电路输出与预期的依赖传播语义一致。作者还在Lean中实现并验证电路求值器，并为未压缩的简单树形受限片段建立桥接结果：满足形式化路由与解除条件的有效DLDS，其抽取路径会被相应电路接受。

Result: 理论上，所构造布尔电路能够在逐路径层面忠实反映DLDS的依赖传播语义。形式化方面，Lean提供了电路求值器的机器检验保证，并证明了简单树形片段中有效DLDS与电路接受之间的受限对应关系。摘要未说明一般压缩DLDS上的完整全局充要性、复杂度界限或实验评估。

Conclusion: 该工作为压缩自然演绎证明的电路化验证提供了形式化基础：它确认了电路编码在局部路径语义上的正确性，并以Lean加强可信度。其主要局限是桥接定理目前仅覆盖未压缩简单树形片段；将结果扩展到一般DLDS、建立全局正确性与复杂度分析，是后续走向实用自动证明认证的关键。

Abstract: In this article, we present a method for encoding Dag-Like Derivability Structures (DLDS), obtained via horizontal compression of Natural Deduction proofs in purely implicational minimal logic, as Boolean circuits. These DLDS compress Natural Deduction tree-like proofs into directed acyclic graphs, preserving logical correctness while reducing redundancy. We formally define the circuit construction process and establish its pointwise correctness, showing that, for any fixed path assignment, the resulting Boolean circuit agrees with the intended dependency-propagation semantics. A Lean formalization establishes machine-checked guarantees for the circuit evaluator and includes a restricted bridge for the uncompressed simple-tree fragment, connecting valid DLDS instances in that fragment to genuine circuit acceptance of their extracted paths under the route and discharge conditions formalized in Lean. This approach opens new perspectives for automated theorem proving and formal certification.

</details>


### [103] [Phase Semantic Cut-elimination for Intuitionistic Linear Logic with Least and Greatest Fixed Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20187)
*Jun Suzuki,Charles Grellois,Katsuhiko Sano*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文针对含最小与最大不动点的直觉主义命题乘加线性逻辑μIMALL，利用相位语义建立了切消定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作提出了带不动点的相关线性逻辑系统及其直觉主义片段，但该片段的切消性质尚未得到证明。论文旨在填补这一理论空白，并将相位语义方法推广到μIMALL。

Method: 作者定义μIMALL及其相位模型，证明两项关键元定理：其一为可靠性，即μIMALL中可证明的公式在所有相位模型中均成立；其二为无切完备性，即在所有相位模型中成立的公式均可在不使用Cut规则的情况下于μIMALL中证明。证明过程在既有针对线性逻辑和含不动点命题片段的相位语义论证基础上进行细化。

Result: 通过可靠性与无切完备性的组合，论文推出μIMALL的切消定理：任何可证明公式都存在不含Cut规则的证明。

Conclusion: 该工作为含最小、最大不动点的直觉主义命题乘加线性逻辑提供了首个基于相位语义的切消证明，补充了该逻辑片段的重要证明论基础，并表明相位语义可有效处理此类不动点结构。

Abstract: This paper establishes the cut-elimination theorem for intuitionistic propositional multiplicative-additive linear logic with the least and greatest fixpoints ($μ$IMALL) by means of its phase semantics. A classical first-order multiplicative-additive linear logic system with the least and greatest fixpoints was introduced by Baelde and Miller (2007). Its intuitionistic fragment was discussed in Baelde (2012), but the cut-elimination theorem for this fragment has not yet been proved. We introduce a propositional fragment of this system, $μ$IMALL, and establish the cut-elimination theorem. To prove the theorem, we define phase semantics for $μ$IMALL and show the following two statements: (1) Soundness: if a formula is provable in $μ$IMALL, then it is true in all phase models, and (2) Cut-free Completeness: if a formula is true in all phase models, then it is provable in $μ$IMALL without Cut. Okada (1999, 2002) employed a phase semantic method to prove the cut-elimination theorems for classical and intuitionistic linear logic systems. De et al. (2022) applied this method to a propositional fragment of classical propositional multiplicative-additive linear logic with the least and greatest fixpoints. We refine and apply their arguments to prove the cut-elimination theorem for $μ$IMALL.

</details>


### [104] [A Machine-checked Proof of Consistency for Impredicative Pure Type Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20188)
*Sebastián Urciuoli*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文报告了在 Agda 中对纯类型系统关键元理论的机械化验证进展，采用经典语法与 Stoughton 多重代换，并完成合流性、主题保持及部分系统一致性的形式化证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型理论的元理论证明通常涉及变量绑定、α-等价和代换等复杂问题，人工证明易出现细节遗漏。作者旨在评估一种基于经典语法和多重代换的机械化方案是否可行，并改善相关证明的清晰性与可复用性。

Method: 论文以经典具名语法表示项和变量，使用 Stoughton 的多重代换处理代换操作；针对合流性，引入并发展 α-可交换关系理论；采用 Takahashi 对 Tait 与 Martin-Löf 方法的修订证明 β-归约合流；对整个纯类型系统族证明主题保持；在假设规范化的前提下，证明某个非谓词化子类的一致性。全部定义与证明均由 Agda 机器检查。

Result: 作者获得了 β-归约合流性的形式化证明、覆盖整个纯类型系统族的主题保持证明，以及针对部分非谓词化系统的一致性结果。α-可交换关系为涉及 α-等价的证明提供了更清晰的组织方式。研究同时总结了该机械化路线在表达与证明管理方面的优点和局限。

Conclusion: 基于经典语法和多重代换的方案能够支持纯类型系统重要元理论性质的端到端机器验证。该方法避免了某些替代表示技术的复杂性，并借助 α-可交换关系改善了合流性证明的表述；但一致性结论仍依赖规范化假设，且方法的适用成本与局限仍需在更广泛的形式化开发中进一步评估。

Abstract: In this paper we continue assessing the feasibility of the approach to the mechanization of type theory by using classical syntax and Stoughton's multiple substitutions and report some substantial progress. We present formal proofs of confluence for beta-reduction and by using Takahashi's revision of Tait and Martin-Löf's proof, subject reduction for the entire family of the Pure Type Systems and consistency for some impredicative subclass, assuming normalization. As to the proof of confluence, we also develop a theory of alpha-commutative relations which, in our view, entails a clearer presentation and treatment of the problem than in similar developments. Finally, we assess general merits and drawbacks of the approach. The whole development has been machine-checked using Agda.

</details>


### [105] [A ProbLog program to infer individual genotypes from familial phenotypes in autosomal, X-linked, and Y-linked Mendelian disorders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20250)
*Maxime Mahout*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出基于ProbLog的概率逻辑程序mendelprob.pl，用于根据多代家系成员的基因型和表型信息，自动推断单基因遗传性状的遗传概率及最可能家系历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗传咨询中需要依据家系史和孟德尔遗传规律评估个体携带或传递先天性疾病的风险，但家系信息重建往往复杂。现有工具对表型信息利用有限，且对不同染色体遗传模式的表达不够直接。

Method: 作者将双等位基因的孟德尔遗传规则编码为ProbLog概率逻辑模型，支持常染色体、X连锁和Y连锁三类遗传模式。用户输入患者及亲属的已知基因型和表型后，模型跨多代计算基因型与表型的后验概率，并据此识别最可能的遗传家系结构。

Result: 在文献及遗传咨询手册中的家系实例上，该方法能够依据家族基因型信息正确推断个体基因型概率，结果与pedprobr一致。同时，该方法还能整合家族表型证据，并能在逻辑模型中直接区分三种遗传模式，这是pedprobr不具备或不易实现的能力。

Conclusion: mendelprob.pl为单基因遗传风险评估提供了可解释、开放且可复用的形式化工具。概率逻辑编程能够结合基因型与表型家系信息，提升遗传咨询中的自动化推断能力，并减少对封闭软件的依赖。

Abstract: The automated reconstruction of patient family history is a common challenge in genetic counseling for disease prevention. Such a family history is usually determined for a particular subset of diseases that are Mendelian, i.e. monogenic, and classified into three categories depending on the chromosome the gene is located: autosomal, X-linked or Y-linked. Mendel's inheritance laws allow for simple probabilistic modeling of the genetic transmission of monogenic disorders. Genetic counsellors use knowledge about the patient's family history and Mendelian laws for assessing risks of transmitting or inheriting congenital conditions. We present mendelprob.pl, a probabilistic logic programming algorithm in ProbLog for deriving probabilities of inheritance of genotypes and phenotypes for genes with two alleles through multiple generations. In particular, the user can input genotypes and phenotypes for a patient and its family, and automatically determine the most probable genetic family history.
