We illustrate the ProbLog model on practical examples of patient pedigrees from the literature and from a genetic counseling handbook. We show that our method correctly infers probability of individual genotypes from knowledge about familial genotypes, yielding the same results as tool pedprobr. However, unlike pedprobr, our approach can exploit knowledge about familial phenotypes. It can also directly distinguish between autosomal, X-linked, and Y-linked disorders, using its intuitive logical modelling. We provide our ProbLog tool for free and open-source on GitHub, making it easily available for genetic counsellors. We conclude on the importance of providing explainable formal methods for a task that clinicians might want to perform using proprietary software.

</details>


### [106] [An Approach to the Abstract Interpretation of Goal-Directed Answer Set Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20252)
*Daniel Jurjo-Rivas,Joaquín Arias,Gopal Gupta,Jose F. Morales,Pedro López-García,Manuel V. Hermenegildo*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文首次将抽象解释系统化地应用于目标导向式答案集编程，并在s(CASP)中实现和验证其静态分析价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抽象解释已成功用于约束逻辑编程中的确定性、类型、别名和资源分析，但此前尚未针对目标导向式答案集编程开展研究。该工作旨在填补这一空白，并利用编译期分析改善程序求值效率与可靠性。

Method: 作者提出一种基于PLAI不动点计算的自顶向下分析算法，并将其实现于Ciao Prolog Preprocessor的抽象解释器中。论文还设计了Shared-Constraints抽象域，用于刻画约束可能诱导的变量关系；随后在s(CASP)上通过多个应用场景评估该方法。

Result: 实验和应用表明，该分析框架可用于检测错误的奇数否定循环、借助Shared-Constraints域提升forall求值效率，以及执行抽象特化，包括简化所需的全局约束。

Conclusion: 编译期抽象解释能够有效增强目标导向式答案集程序的静态分析与运行时求值。该研究为ASP中的类型、约束关系、特化和验证等后续分析技术提供了实现基础。

Abstract: Abstract Interpretation infers and verifies program properties by over-approximating program semantics. It has been highly successful for (Constraint) Logic Programming, enabling the analysis of determinism, types, aliasing, and resource usage, as well as application in verification and program optimization. However, Abstract Interpretation has not yet been studied in the context of Goal Directed Answer Set Programming (ASP). In this work, we take a first step in this direction. We present a top-down algorithm based on the PLAI fixpoint, implemented in the abstract interpreter of the Ciao Prolog Preprocessor, to perform abstract interpretation of goal-directed ASP. We also introduce the Shared-Constraints abstract domain, designed to capture potential relations among variables induced by constraints. Finally, we study the practicality of the approach in s(CASP) through three applications: detection of false odd loops over negation, efficient forall evaluation enabled by the Shared-Constraints domain, and abstract specialization (including the simplification of required global constraints). Our results show that compile-time static analysis can improve the evaluation of goal-directed ASP programs.

</details>


### [107] [ASPIC: Proof-of-Concept ASP to Picat Transpiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20254)
*Cristian Grozea,Marius Popescu*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出ASPIC原型库，将扩展ASP-Core-2程序转换为Picat谓词，并利用Picat内置SAT求解器求解，实现ASP与Picat的双向嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASP建模与求解通常受限于其自身语言和约束表达能力。作者旨在结合ASP的声明式规则建模与Picat的编程、函数及有限域约束能力，以支持更灵活的混合建模。

Method: ASPIC将ASP-Core-2扩展语法编译为Picat谓词；生成的程序既可直接由Picat SAT求解器处理，也可嵌入更大的Picat程序。与此同时，ASP规则中可调用Picat谓词和函数，从而形成“ASP in Picat”与“Picat in ASP”的集成方式。

Result: 初步测试显示：对于不存在正循环、且未使用特殊Picat特性的程序，ASPIC与clingo具有良好兼容性。借助Picat嵌入能力，系统可同时使用ASP原子和有限域变量进行高效建模，并可表达非线性约束。

Conclusion: ASPIC证明了将ASP编译并嵌入Picat的可行性，为ASP、SAT求解和约束编程的融合提供了原型实现；其当前兼容性范围主要受无正循环等条件限制。

Abstract: This article presents ASPIC, a new proof-of-concept library that converts extended syntax ASP-Core-2 programs to Picat predicates  that can be solved right away with the integrated Picat SAT solver, or embedded in larger Picat programs ("ASP in Picat"), and that can in turn make use of various Picat predicates and functions ("Picat in ASP"). The first tests prove good compatibility with clingo, on programs lacking positive loops and when the special Picat features are not used. With the embedded Picat, it touches the application field of clingcon as well, by being able to efficiently model with both ASP atoms and with finite domain variables, but goes beyond that by being able to model also non-linear constraints.

</details>


### [108] [Connectivity at the crossroad of intuitionistic and classical polarizations in linear logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20303)
*Raffaele Di Donna,Giulio Guerrieri,Lorenzo Tortora de Falco*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出线性逻辑片段 VMELL，使一种原本仅为必要条件的图性质成为证明结构正确性的充分且可高效判定的标准，并将其与 bang calculus 及按名/按值 λ 演算的线性逻辑翻译联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在乘法指数线性逻辑（MELL）中，正确性图的无环性及其连通分量数量条件虽是证明结构可对应序列演算证明的必要条件，却并不充分。因此，需要识别一个具有良好表达能力的 MELL 子片段，使该简单图条件能够完整刻画证明网的正确性。

Method: 作者对证明结构施加几何限制，定义 VMELL 片段。随后证明：在 VMELL 中，正确性图无环，且连通分量数恰比 bottom 或 weakening 节点数多一，这一性质等价于证明结构的正确性。论文还将 bang calculus 项翻译为 VMELL 证明网，分解按名调用与按值调用 λ 演算到线性逻辑的常规翻译，并建立割消去与 bang 规约之间的模拟关系。

Result: VMELL 成为一个可计算高效验证的 MELL 片段，其中所研究的图性质构成充要的正确性判据。该片段统一了经典与直觉主义极化形式；bang calculus 的项可被显式刻画为 VMELL 证明网；同时，证明网中的割消去能够模拟 bang calculus 的规约。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是通过 VMELL 将简单的组合图条件提升为可靠且高效的证明网正确性标准，并以此为基础建立 bang calculus、按名/按值 λ 演算和线性逻辑证明网之间的统一语义与规约对应。

Abstract: We investigate a property that extends the Danos-Regnier correctness criterion for linear logic proof-structures. The property applies to the correctness graphs of a proof-structure: it states that any such graph is acyclic and the number of its connected components is exactly one more than the number of nodes bottom or weakening. This is known to be necessary but not sufficient in multiplicative exponential linear logic (MELL) to recover a sequent calculus proof from a proof-structure. We present a geometric restriction on proof-structures allowing us to turn this necessary property into a sufficient one, computationally efficient: we can thus introduce the notable fragment VMELL of MELL for which the property is indeed a correctness criterion. The fragment VMELL brings together the classical and intuitionistic polarizations. We translate the bang calculus terms into proof-nets of VMELL, factorize the usual translations in linear logic of the call-by-name and call-by-value lambda-calculi, prove that cut elimination simulates bang reduction, and provide an explicit characterization of the bang calculus terms as proof-nets.

</details>
